<current_focus>
**CRUD Editor Pagination Architecture** — User wants server-side transparent pagination via a new WFM process `GenericCRUD.Models.QueryPage`. The client (hub) should send only simple parameters (`Model`, `ServiceType`, `Limit`, `Offset`, `Search`, `SortCol`, `SortDir`), and the server dynamically builds `QueryFilter` with `ConditionGroup` based on model config. This keeps hub "dumb" and works uniformly across `Simple`/`DapperSimple`/`History`/`DapperHistory` model types — switching model type only requires changing `Type` in config, zero client changes.

**Rationale:** `QueryFilter` already exists and returns `TotalCount`, but lacks `Offset`. Adding `Offset` to all 4 repository types + handlers makes `QueryFilter` work uniformly for all model types. The new WFM process will translate simple search/sort/page params into `ConditionGroup`/`OrderBy`/`Limit`/`Offset` and call existing `GenericCRUD.Action` with `Action: "QueryFilter"`.
</current_focus>

<completed_tasks>
- **Stage type fix:** `ProcessDiagram.tsx` and `useStageMutations.ts` were creating stages with `*Definition` suffix (`CRUDDefinition`, `CommandDefinition`) instead of short form (`CRUD`, `Command`). Fixed — `WebProcess` uses short types, C# converters add `Definition` suffix.
- **RecordDialog JSON ↔ Field sync:** Implemented bidirectional sync when toggling between "Full JSON edit" and "Field editor".
- **RecordDialog refactor to unified state:** Single `data: Record<string, unknown>` as source of truth instead of separate `fields`/`jsonText` states.
- **Key display in CrudDataTable:** `model.KeyName` now shown as first column with `*` marker.
- **Key handling in RecordDialog:** Key field displayed separately; read-only for `Identity=true` or `Update` mode; restored from `data` on JSON→Field switch and Submit to prevent overwriting.
- **PropertyArrayEditor component:** New UI for editing `CrudProperty[]` with type-dependent validation fields (Pattern/MinLength/MaxLength for strings; Min/Max for numbers; Enums chips).
- **Type-aware field rendering:** `int`/`long` → `number step=1`; `decimal`/`double` → `number step=any`; `bool` → checkbox; `bool?` → select; `DateTime` → `datetime-local`; `Enums` → `<select>` dropdown; `JObject`/`JArray` with nested `Properties` → `PropertyArrayEditor`.
- **Validation in RecordDialog:** Real-time validation for `IsRequired`, `Pattern`, `Min`/`Max`, `MinLength`/`MaxLength`, `Enums`.
- **Delete mode fix:** Validation skipped in delete mode so empty/null fields don't block deletion.
- **DateTime empty string fix:** `buildEmptyRecord` now sets `null` for `DateTime`/`TimeSpan` instead of `""` to prevent `String '' was not recognized as a valid DateTime` server error.
- **Stale model fix after CRUDModel edit:** After successful Add/Update in `CrudEditorPage`, `activeTab.model` is refreshed from response so new Enums/Properties are immediately visible.
</completed_tasks>

<active_issues>

WFM process that validates and routes CRUD actions. Supports actions: Config, Add, AddList, Delete, DeleteAll, DeleteList, Get, GetAll, GetList, Query, QueryFilter, Update, UpdateList, ChangeActivity, GetHistory, GetHistories.

**Key elements:**

<file>
`SalGenericCRUDHandler/Implementations/Conditions/QueryExtensions.cs`

**Summary:**
SQL/LINQ query builders for `QueryFilter`. `QuerySimpleAsync`/`QueryHistoryAsync` use SQL `LIMIT` but **no `OFFSET`**. `QuerySimple` (LINQ for cache) uses `.Take(limit)` but **no `.Skip(offset)`**.

**Key elements:**
- `QuerySimpleAsync<TEntity,TKey>` — SQL with `AddLimit(AddOrderBy(...), limit)`
- `QuerySimple<TEntity,TKey>` (LINQ) — `.Take(limit)` only
- `QuerySimpleCountAsync` / `QueryHistoryCountAsync` — returns `TotalCount`

**Required change:** Add `offset` parameter and `AddLimitOffset(sql, limit, offset)` → SQL: `limit {limit} offset {offset}`; LINQ: `.Skip(offset).Take(limit)`.
</file>

**Summary:**
Auto-generated handler code for `QueryFilter` command. Parses `Filter` (JConditionGroup/ConditionGroup), `OrderBy`, `Limit`, `ReturnProperties` from command.

**Current code:**
```csharp
var group   = command["Filter"].ConvertValue<JConditionGroup>();
var limit   = ... ?? 100;
var orderBy = command["OrderBy"].ConvertValue<OrderBy>();
var returnProperties = ...;
var (models, totalCount) = await repository.QueryFilterAsync(group, orderBy, limit, returnProperties);
```

**Required change:** Add `var offset = command["Offset"]?.ConvertValue<int?>() ?? 0;` and pass to `QueryFilterAsync`.

<file>
`SalGenericCRUDHandler/Implementations/Validation/DtoValidation.cs`

**Summary:**
Server-side validation logic per type. Determines which `PropertyValidation` fields apply:

- `string` → MinLength, MaxLength, Pattern, Enums
- `int`/`long`/`decimal`/`double` → Min, Max, Pattern, Enums
- `DateTime` → Pattern only
- `TimeSpan`, `Guid`, `JObject`, `JArray`, `bool` → none (IsRequired only)
- Nullable numeric types (`int?`, `long?`, etc.) **not handled in switch** — only IsRequired works.

**Key element:** `ValidateString`, `ValidateNumber`, `ValidateDateTime` methods.

<important_context>
- **ModelConfig types:** `Simple` (EF Core + cache), `History` (EF Core + cache with details), `DapperSimple` (Dapper + PostgreSQL), `DapperHistory` (Dapper + PostgreSQL with details). Switching between them is a config change only; clients must not know the difference.
- **QueryFilter already returns `TotalCount`:** The tuple `(IEnumerable<JObject>, int)` from `QueryFilterAsync` includes total count. Hub just needs `Offset` added to paginate.
- **Search strategy (proposed for `QueryPage` process):** Server should build `ConditionGroup` with `Operator: "Or"` and `Compare: "Contains"` across all string/text properties when `Search` param provided. This avoids client building complex filters.
- **Client should remain "dumb":** User explicitly rejected hub building `ConditionGroup`. Proposed `QueryPage` process accepts: `Model`, `ServiceType`, `Limit`, `Offset`, `Search?`, `SortCol?`, `SortDir?`.
- **DateTime empty string issue resolved:** `buildEmptyRecord` now returns `null` for DateTime fields. Server receives `null` instead of `""`, avoiding `String '' was not recognized as a valid DateTime`.
- **Enums refresh issue resolved:** After editing `CRUDModel` properties (e.g., adding Enums to `BackProcessHandler`), the active tab's model is refreshed so Enums appear immediately without page reload.
</important_context>

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

Пользователь хочет, чтобы вся логика фильтрации и пагинации была на сервере. Клиент (hub) должен быть "тупым" — просто шлёт `Model`, `Limit`, `Offset`, `Search`, `SortCol`, `SortDir`. А сервер сам строит `QueryFilter` динамически, зная конфиг модели.

Это правильный подход. Нужно создать новый WFM процесс `GenericCRUD.Models.QueryPage`, который:

1. Получает `Model` (имя модели)
2. Получает `Limit`, `Offset`
3. Получает `Search` (строка поиска)
4. Получает `SortCol`, `SortDir`
5. Загружает `ModelConfig` через `GenericCRUD.Action` с `Action: "Config"`
6. Строит `QueryFilter` динамически:
   - `Filter` — если `Search` не пустой, создаёт `ConditionGroup` с `Or` оператором по всем текстовым полям модели (`Contains`/`Like`)
   - `OrderBy` — по `SortCol` и `SortDir`
   - `Limit` — из входных данных
   - `Offset` — из входных данных
7. Вызывает `GenericCRUD.Action` с `Action: "QueryFilter"` и построенными данными
8. Возвращает результат `{ Models, TotalCount }` клиенту

Процесс будет похож на `GenericCRUDModelsAction`, но специализированный только для `QueryPage`.

Вход:
```json
{
  "Model": "FrontProcessHandler",
  "Limit": 25,
  "Offset": 0,
  "Search": "foo",
  "SortCol": "Id",
  "SortDir": "desc"
}
```

Внутри процесса:
1. `Config` — получаем `ModelConfig`
2. `BuildQueryFilter` — строим `Filter`, `OrderBy`
3. `QueryFilter` — вызываем `GenericCRUD.Action` с `Action: "QueryFilter"`

Выход:
```json
{
  "Models": [...],
  "TotalCount": 147
}
```

Это очень чистый подход. Клиент вообще не знает про `ConditionGroup`, `QueryFilter`, типы моделей.

Нужно написать этот процесс. Но процессы в WFM — это C# файлы в `WFM.Sources/PROCESS/`.

Давайте создадим `GenericCRUDModelsQueryPage.cs`.

Структура процесса:
```csharp
using System;
using System.Linq;
using Newtonsoft.Json.Linq;
using SAL.API;
using SalGenericCRUDHandler.Settings;
using SalGenericCRUDHandler.Implementations.Conditions;
using WFM.Common.Definitions.Process;
using WFM.Common.Definitions.Stages.Definitions;

namespace WFM.Sources.PROCESS;

public class GenericCRUDModelsQueryPageInitObject
{
    public string Model { get; set; }
    public int? Limit { get; set; }
    public int? Offset { get; set; }
    public string Search { get; set; }
    public string SortCol { get; set; }
    public string SortDir { get; set; }
}

public class GenericCRUDModelsQueryPageContext
{
    public ModelConfig Config { get; set; }
    public JObject Result { get; set; }
}

public class GenericCRUDModelsQueryPageProcessResult
{
}

[Process("GenericCRUD.Models.QueryPage")]
public class GenericCRUDModelsQueryPage : BaseTypedProcess<GenericCRUDModelsQueryPageInitObject, GenericCRUDModelsQueryPageContext, GenericCRUDModelsQueryPageProcessResult>, IProcessScript
{
    public GenericCRUDModelsQueryPage(JToken initObject, JObject session) : base(initObject, session) { }

    public StartDefinition Start() => new("Start")
    {
        GetNextStage = () => Config
    };

    public EndDefinition Success() => new("Success")
    {
        GetData = (stage) => SuccessResult(Context.Result)
    };

    public EndDefinition Failed() => new("Failed")
    {
        GetData = (stage) => FailedResult(Error)
    };

    public CRUDDefinition Config() => new("Get Model Config")
    {
        GetData = (stage) =>
        {
            stage.Model = InitObject.Model;
            stage.Action = "Config";
            return new { };
        },
        GetNextStage = () =>
        {
            Context.Config = Result.ToObject<ModelConfig>();
            return QueryPage;
        },
        GetNextErrorStage = () => Failed
    };

    public CRUDDefinition QueryPage() => new("Query Page")
    {
        GetData = (stage) =>
        {
            stage.Model = InitObject.Model;
            stage.Action = "QueryFilter";

            var limit = InitObject.Limit ?? 25;
            var offset = InitObject.Offset ?? 0;
            var search = InitObject.Search;
            var sortCol = InitObject.SortCol ?? Context.Config.KeyName;
            var sortDir = InitObject.SortDir?.ToLower() == "asc" ? false : true;

            JConditionGroup filter = null;
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(search))
            {
                var textProps = Context.Config.Properties
                    .Where(p => p.Type == "string" || p.Type == "DateTime" || p.Type == "DateTime?")
                    .ToArray();

                if (textProps.Length > 0)
                {
                    filter = new JConditionGroup
                    {
                        Operator = ConditionOperator.Or,
                        Conditions = textProps.Select(p => new JConditionValue
                        {
                            Name = p.Name,
                            Compare = CompareOperator.Contains,
                            Value = search,
                            Type = "string"
                        }).Cast<IJCondition>().ToList()
                    };
                }
            }

            var orderBy = new OrderBy
            {
                Name = sortCol,
                Desc = sortDir
            };

            return JObject.FromObject(new
            {
                Filter = filter,
                Limit = limit,
                Offset = offset,
                OrderBy = orderBy
            });
        },
        GetNextStage = () =>
        {
            Context.Result = Result;
            return Success;
        },
        GetNextErrorStage = () => Failed
    };
}
```

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

Отлично. Теперь я понимаю структуру процесса. 

Нужно создать `GenericCRUDModelsQueryPage.cs`. Процесс будет:
1. `Config` — получаем конфиг модели
2. `QueryPage` — строим `QueryFilter` и вызываем `GenericCRUD.Action`

Входные данные:
- `Model` — string
- `Limit` — int?
- `Offset` — int?
- `Search` — string?
- `SortCol` — string?
- `SortDir` — string? ("asc" / "desc")

`SortCol` по умолчанию = `Config.KeyName`
`SortDir` по умолчанию = "asc"

Для `Search`:
- Если не пустой, строим `ConditionGroup` с `Operator: "Or"` и `Conditions` по всем текстовым свойствам (string, DateTime, DateTime?).
- `Compare: "Contains"`
- `Value: search`
- `Type: "string"` (или тип свойства? Нужно подумать. Для `Contains` можно использовать `string` для всех, так как `Contains` работает как `Like` в SQL, и драйвер PostgreSQL сконвертирует).

Для `SortCol`:
- Если `SortCol` не указан, используем `Config.KeyName`
- Если указан, проверяем, что такое свойство существует в `Config.Properties`? Для безопасности, да — проверим. Если нет — fallback на `KeyName`.

Для `SortDir`:
- "desc" → `Desc = true`
- иначе → `Desc = false`

`Limit` — default 25
`Offset` — default 0

Возвращаем:
```json
{
  "Models": [...],
  "TotalCount": 147
}
```

Но подождите, `QueryFilter` уже возвращает `{ Models, TotalCount }`? Давайте посмотрим, что возвращает `QueryFilter` в `SalGenericCRUDHandler`.

</details>

using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using SalGenericCRUDHandler.Implementations.Conditions;

namespace SalGenericCRUDHandler.Definitions
{
    public interface ICRUDRepository<TModel, TEntity, in TKey> where TEntity : IEntity<TKey>
    {
        public Task<TModel>                      AddAsync(TModel                  model,  string[] returnProperties);
        public Task<IEnumerable<TModel>>         AddListAsync(TModel[]            models, string[] returnProperties);
        public Task<IEnumerable<TModel>>         GetAllAsync(string[]             returnProperties);
        public Task<IEnumerable<TModel>>         GetAllAsync(int                  limit,    string[] returnProperties);
        public Task<IEnumerable<TModel>>         GetListAsync(TKey[]              ids,      string[] returnProperties);
        public Task<TModel>                      GetAsync(TKey                    entityId, string[] returnProperties);
        public Task<(IEnumerable<TModel>, int )> QueryFilterAsync(ConditionGroup  group,    OrderBy  orderBy, int      limit, string[] returnProperties);
        public Task<(IEnumerable<TModel>, int )> QueryFilterAsync(JConditionGroup group,    OrderBy  orderBy, int      limit, string[] returnProperties);
        public Task<TModel>                      UpdateAsync(TKey                 entityId, TModel   model,   string[] returnProperties);
        public Task<IEnumerable<TModel>>         UpdateListAsync(TKey[]           ids,      TModel[] model,   string[] returnProperties);
        public Task                              DeleteAsync(TKey                 entityId);
        public Task                              DeleteAllAsync();
        public Task                              DeleteListAsync(TKey[] ids);
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Data;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Dapper;
using SalGenericCRUDHandler.Definitions;
using SalGenericCRUDHandler.Settings;
using SalGenericCRUDHandler.Syntax.Common;

namespace SalGenericCRUDHandler.Implementations.Conditions
{
    public static class QueryExtensions
    {
        public static async Task<IEnumerable<TEntity>> QueryHistoryAsync<TEntity, TDetailEntity, TKey>(this IDbConnection db, ModelConfig config, ConditionGroup group, OrderBy orderBy, int limit, IDbTransaction transaction = null) where TEntity : class, IHistoryEntity<TDetailEntity, TKey>
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       where TDetailEntity : class, IHistoryDetailEntity<TKey>
        {
            AddTablePrefix(group,   config);
            AddTablePrefix(orderBy, config);

            var (sql, parameters) = GetSelectSql(config, "History", group);
            sql                   = AddLimit(AddOrderBy(sql, orderBy), limit);

            return await db.QueryAsync<TEntity, TDetailEntity, TEntity>(sql, (e, d) =>
            {
                e.Details = d;

                return e;
            }, parameters, splitOn: "DetailId", transaction: transaction);
        }

        public static async Task<IEnumerable<TEntity>> QuerySimpleAsync<TEntity, TKey>(this IDbConnection db, ModelConfig config, ConditionGroup group, OrderBy orderBy, int limit, IDbTransaction transaction = null) where TEntity : class, IEntity<TKey>
        {
            var (sql, parameters) = GetSelectSql(config, "Simple", group);
            sql                   = AddLimit(AddOrderBy(sql, orderBy), limit);

            return await db.QueryAsync<TEntity>(sql, parameters, transaction);
        }


        public static (IEnumerable<TEntity> entities, int totalCount) QuerySimple<TEntity, TKey>(this IEnumerable<TEntity> collection, ModelConfig config, JConditionGroup group, OrderBy orderBy, int limit) where TEntity : class, IEntity<TKey>
        {
            var entities = collection.Where(x => group?.Predicate(x, config) ?? true);

            var result = orderBy switch
            {
                { Desc: true } o  => entities.OrderByDescending(x => x.GetType().GetProperty(o.Name).GetValue(x, null)).ToArray(),
                { Desc: false } o => entities.OrderBy(x => x.GetType().GetProperty(o.Name).GetValue(x,           null)).ToArray(),

                _ => entities.ToArray(),
            };

            var totalCount = result.Length;

            return (limit == -1 ? result : result.Take(limit), totalCount);
        }

        public static async Task<int> QueryHistoryCountAsync(this IDbConnection db, ModelConfig config, ConditionGroup group, IDbTransaction transaction = null)
        {
            var (sql, parameters) = GetSelectSql(config, "HistoryCount", group);

            return await db.ExecuteScalarAsync<int>(sql, parameters, transaction);
        }

        public static async Task<int> QuerySimpleCountAsync(this IDbConnection db, ModelConfig config, ConditionGroup group, IDbTransaction transaction = null)
        {
            var (sql, parameters) = GetSelectSql(config, "SimpleCount", group);

            return await db.ExecuteScalarAsync<int>(sql, parameters, transaction);
        }

        private static ( string sql, DynamicParameters parameters) GetSelectSql(ModelConfig config, string type, ICondition group = null)
        {
            if (group == null)
                return ($"{GetSelect(config, type)}", null);

            var parameters = new DynamicParameters();
            var statements = group.GetStatement(parameters);

            return ($"{GetSelect(config, type)} where {statements}", parameters);
        }

        private static string GetSelect(ModelConfig config, string type) => type switch
        {
            "History"      => GetHistorySelect(config),
            "HistoryCount" => GetHistorySelect(config).Replace($"{config.KeyName} as Id,*", "count(1)"),
            "Simple"       => GetSimpleSelect(config),
            "SimpleCount"  => GetSimpleSelect(config).Replace($"{config.KeyName} as Id,*", "count(1)"),
            _              => throw new Exception("")
        };


        private static string GetHistorySelect(ModelConfig config) => $"select {config.KeyName} as Id,* from \"{config.ToTable()}\" t join \"{config.ToTable()}_details\" td on t.detailid = td.detailid";

        private static string GetSimpleSelect(ModelConfig config) => $"select {config.KeyName} as Id,* from \"{config.ToTable()}\"";

        private static string AddLimit(string sql, int limit)
        {
            return limit == -1 ? sql : $"{sql} limit {limit}";
        }

        private static string AddOrderBy(string sql, OrderBy order = null)
        {
            if (order == null)
                return sql;

            var desc = order.Desc ? "desc" : string.Empty;

            return $"{sql} order by {order.Name} {desc}";
        }

        private static void AddTablePrefix(IGroupCondition group, ModelConfig config)
        {
            if (group == null)
                return;

            foreach (var condition in group.Conditions)
            {
                switch (condition)
                {
                    case ConditionValue value:
                        value.Name = AddTablePrefix(config, value.Name);

                        break;

                    case ConditionGroup childGroup:
                        AddTablePrefix(childGroup, config);

                        break;
                }
            }
        }

        private static void AddTablePrefix(OrderBy orderBy, ModelConfig config)
        {
            if (orderBy == null)
                return;

            orderBy.Name = AddTablePrefix(config, orderBy.Name);
        }

        private static string AddTablePrefix(ModelConfig config, string name)
        {
            return config.Properties.Select(x => x.Name.ToLower()).Contains(name.ToLower()) ? $"td.{name}" : $"t.{name}";
        }
    }
}


</details>

using System;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Autofac;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Newtonsoft.Json.Linq;
using Npgsql;
using SAL.API;
using SalGenericCRUDHandler.Definitions;
using SalGenericCRUDHandler.Implementations.Conditions;
using SalGenericCRUDHandler.Infrastructure;
using SalGenericCRUDHandler.Settings;

namespace SalGenericCRUDHandler.Implementations
{
    public abstract class BaseCRUDSimpleRepository<TEntity, TKey> : ICRUDSimpleRepository<JObject, TEntity, TKey> where TEntity : class, IEntity<TKey>
    {
        private readonly   ModelConfig                  _config;
        private readonly   MetadataReference            _metadata;
        private            ICacheService<TKey, TEntity> _cacheService;
        public abstract    DbContext                    CreateContext();
        protected abstract TEntity                      CreateEntity(JObject     model);
        protected abstract TEntity                      UpdateEntity(JObject     model,  TEntity   entity);
        protected abstract TEntity[]                    UpdateEntities(JObject[] model,  TEntity[] entities);
        protected abstract JObject                      ToModel(TEntity          entity, string[]  returnProperties);


        protected BaseCRUDSimpleRepository(ModelConfig config, IEnumerable<byte> image)
        {
            _config   = config;
            _metadata = MetadataReference.CreateFromImage(image);
        }

        public async Task InitializeAsync(ContainerBuilder builder)
        {
            try
            {
                builder.RegisterInstance(this).As<ICRUDSimpleRepository<JObject, TEntity, TKey>>();

                await using var context = CreateContext();

                await TableInitializer.CreateIfNotExistAsync(context, _config.Table);

                var entities = await context.Set<TEntity>().ToArrayAsync();
                _cacheService = new CacheService<TKey, TEntity>(new ConcurrentDictionary<TKey, TEntity>(entities.ToDictionary(x => x.Id)), NotFound);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"InitializeAsync error =>", e);

                throw;
            }
        }

        public async Task InitializeRuntimeAsync()
        {
            await using var context = CreateContext();
            await TableInitializer.CreateIfNotExistAsync(context, _config.Table);
            var entities = await context.Set<TEntity>().ToArrayAsync();
            _cacheService = new CacheService<TKey, TEntity>(new ConcurrentDictionary<TKey, TEntity>(entities.ToDictionary(x => x.Id)), NotFound);
        }

        public async Task<JObject> AddAsync(JObject model, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            await using var context     = CreateContext();
            await using var transaction = await context.Database.BeginTransactionAsync();

            try
            {
                var entity = CreateEntity(model);

                await context.Set<TEntity>().AddAsync(entity);
                await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                _cacheService.Upsert(entity.Id, entity);

                await transaction.CommitAsync();

                return ToModel(entity, returnProperties);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                await transaction.RollbackAsync();

                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public async Task<IEnumerable<JObject>> AddListAsync(JObject[] models, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            await using var context     = CreateContext();
            await using var transaction = await context.Database.BeginTransactionAsync();

            try
            {
                var entities = models.Select(CreateEntity).ToArray();


                await context.Set<TEntity>().AddRangeAsync(entities);
                await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                foreach (var entity in entities)
                    _cacheService.Upsert(entity.Id, entity);

                await transaction.CommitAsync();

                return entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                await transaction.RollbackAsync();

                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }
        //READ

        public Task<IEnumerable<JObject>> GetListAsync(TKey[] ids, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            try
            {
                var entities = _cacheService.GetAll().Where(x => ids.Contains(x.Id));

                return Task.FromResult(entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties)));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public Task<JObject> GetAsync(TKey entityId, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            try
            {
                return Task.FromResult(ToModel(_cacheService.Get(entityId), returnProperties));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }


        public Task<IEnumerable<JObject>> GetAllAsync(string[] returnProperties)
        {
            try
            {
                var entities = _cacheService.GetAll();

                return Task.FromResult(entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties)));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public Task<IEnumerable<JObject>> GetAllAsync(int limit, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            try
            {
                var entities = _cacheService.GetAll().Take(limit);

                return Task.FromResult(entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties)));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }


        public async Task<(IEnumerable<JObject>, int)> QueryAsync(string query, OrderBy orderBy, int limit, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            try
            {
                var predicate = await GenericQuery.CreatePredicate<TEntity, TKey>(query, _metadata);
                var (entities, totalCount) = _cacheService.Query(predicate, orderBy, limit);

                return (entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties)), totalCount);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public Task<IEnumerable<JObject>> QueryAsync(Func<TEntity, bool> predicate, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            try
            {
                var (entities, _) = _cacheService.Query(predicate, null, 0);

                return Task.FromResult(entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties)));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public Task<(IEnumerable<JObject>, int)> QueryFilterAsync(ConditionGroup group, OrderBy orderBy, int limit, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            throw new NotImplementedException();
        }

        public Task<(IEnumerable<JObject>, int)> QueryFilterAsync(JConditionGroup group, OrderBy orderBy, int limit, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            try
            {
                var cashEntities = _cacheService.GetAll().ToArray();

                var result = cashEntities.QuerySimple<TEntity, TKey>(_config, group, orderBy, limit);

                return Task.FromResult((result.entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties)), result.totalCount));
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw GetException(ex);
            }
        }
        public Task<long> QueryFilterCountAsync(JConditionGroup group) => throw new NotImplementedException();

        //UPDATE
        public async Task<JObject> UpdateAsync(TKey entityId, JObject model, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            try
            {
                await using var context = CreateContext();
                var             entity  = await context.Set<TEntity>().FirstOrDefaultAsync(x => x.Id.Equals(entityId)) ?? throw NotFound();

                UpdateEntity(model, entity);

                await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                entity = _cacheService.Upsert(entityId, entity);

                return ToModel(entity, returnProperties);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public async Task<IEnumerable<JObject>> UpdateListAsync(TKey[] ids, JObject[] models, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            try
            {
                await using var context  = CreateContext();
                var             entities = await context.Set<TEntity>().Where(x => ids.Contains(x.Id)).ToArrayAsync();

                UpdateEntities(models, entities);

                await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                foreach (var entity in entities)
                    _cacheService.Upsert(entity.Id, entity);

                return entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }


        //DELETE
        public async Task DeleteAsync(TKey entityId)
        {
            try
            {
                await using var context = CreateContext();
                var             entity  = await context.Set<TEntity>().FirstOrDefaultAsync(x => x.Id.Equals(entityId)) ?? throw NotFound();

                context.Set<TEntity>().Remove(entity);

                await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                _cacheService.Delete(entity.Id);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public async Task DeleteAllAsync()
        {
            try
            {
                await using var context  = CreateContext();
                var             entities = await context.Set<TEntity>().ToArrayAsync();

                context.Set<TEntity>().RemoveRange(entities);

                await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                foreach (var entity in entities)
                    _cacheService.Delete(entity.Id);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public async Task DeleteListAsync(TKey[] ids)
        {
            try
            {
                await using var context  = CreateContext();
                var             entities = await context.Set<TEntity>().Where(x => ids.Contains(x.Id)).ToArrayAsync();

                context.Set<TEntity>().RemoveRange(entities);

                await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                foreach (var entity in entities)
                    _cacheService.Delete(entity.Id);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }


        private static Exception GetException(Exception e) => e switch
        {
            DbUpdateException ue => ue.InnerException switch
            {
                PostgresException { SqlState: PostgresErrorCodes.UniqueViolation } pe => Duplicate(pe),
                _                                                                     => Error(e)
            },
            SalException => e,
            _            => Error(e)
        };

        private static Exception    NotFound()             => SalError.CreateException($"{typeof(TEntity).Name}NotFound",  $"�-������?�? �?�� �?�����?��?��");
        private static Exception    Duplicate(Exception e) => SalError.CreateException($"{typeof(TEntity).Name}Duplicate", $"�-������?�? �?��� �?�?�%��?�'�?�?��' ", innerException: e);
        private static SalException Error(Exception     e) => SalError.CreateException("Error",                            e.Message,                 innerException: e);
    }
}

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

Теперь я вижу картину полностью.

**План:**

1. **Добавить `Offset` в `QueryExtensions.cs`:**
   - `AddLimit` → `AddLimitOffset(string sql, int limit, int offset)`
   - `QuerySimpleAsync` — добавить `offset` параметр, вызывать `AddLimitOffset`
   - `QueryHistoryAsync` — добавить `offset` параметр, вызывать `AddLimitOffset`
   - `QuerySimple<TEntity,TKey>` (LINQ) — добавить `offset` параметр, использовать `.Skip(offset).Take(limit)`

2. **Добавить `Offset` в `ICRUDRepository`:**

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

Давайте обновим `QueryExtensions.cs`. Нужно добавить `offset` параметр в `QuerySimpleAsync`, `QueryHistoryAsync` и `QuerySimple` (LINQ).

Также нужно заменить `AddLimit` на `AddLimitOffset` или добавить перегрузку.

Смотрим текущий `QueryExtensions.cs`:
- `QueryHistoryAsync` — `int limit`, использует `AddLimit(AddOrderBy(...), limit)`
- `QuerySimpleAsync` — `int limit`, использует `AddLimit(AddOrderBy(...), limit)`
- `QuerySimple<TEntity,TKey>` — `int limit`, использует `.Take(limit)`

Для SQL (PostgreSQL): `LIMIT {limit} OFFSET {offset}` (или `limit {limit} offset {offset}`)
Для LINQ: `.Skip(offset).Take(limit)`

Для `totalCount` в `QuerySimple<TEntity,TKey>`: `totalCount` должен считаться ДО `Skip`/`Take`. В текущем коде:
```csharp
var entities = collection.Where(...);
var result = orderBy switch { ... };
var totalCount = result.Length;
return (limit == -1 ? result : result.Take(limit), totalCount);
```

Нужно:
```csharp
var entities = collection.Where(...);
var ordered = orderBy switch { ... };
var totalCount = ordered.Length;
var paged = ordered;
if (offset > 0) paged = paged.Skip(offset);
if (limit != -1) paged = paged.Take(limit);
return (paged.ToArray(), totalCount);
```

Для SQL:
```csharp
private static string AddLimitOffset(string sql, int limit, int offset)
{
    if (limit == -1) return sql;
    return $"{sql} limit {limit} offset {offset}";
}
```

Теперь обновим `QueryExtensions.cs`.

</details>

using System;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Autofac;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Newtonsoft.Json.Linq;
using Npgsql;
using SAL.API;
using SalGenericCRUDHandler.Definitions;
using SalGenericCRUDHandler.Implementations.Conditions;
using SalGenericCRUDHandler.Infrastructure;
using SalGenericCRUDHandler.Settings;

namespace SalGenericCRUDHandler.Implementations
{
    public abstract class BaseCRUDHistoryRepository<TEntity, TDetailEntity, TKey> : ICRUDHistoryRepository<JObject, TEntity, TKey> where TEntity : class, IHistoryEntity<TDetailEntity, TKey>
                                                                                                                                   where TDetailEntity : class, IHistoryDetailEntity
    {
        private            ICacheService<TKey, TEntity> _cacheService;
        private readonly   MetadataReference            _metadata;
        private            ModelConfig                  _config;
        public abstract    DbContext                    CreateContext();
        protected abstract TEntity                      CreateEntity(JObject           model);
        protected abstract TDetailEntity                CreateDetailEntity(JObject     model,  TEntity       entity, bool init);
        protected abstract TEntity                      UpdateEntity(JObject           model,  TEntity       entity);
        protected abstract TDetailEntity                UpdateDetailEntity(JObject     model,  TDetailEntity detailEntity);
        protected abstract TEntity[]                    UpdateEntities(JObject[]       model,  TEntity[]     entities);
        protected abstract TEntity[]                    UpdateCreateEntities(JObject[] models, TEntity[]     entities);
        protected abstract JObject                      ToModel(TEntity                entity, string[]      returnProperties);
        protected abstract JObject                      ToModel(TEntity                entity, TDetailEntity detailEntity, string[] returnProperties);

        protected BaseCRUDHistoryRepository(ModelConfig config, IEnumerable<byte> image)
        {
            _config   = config;
            _metadata = MetadataReference.CreateFromImage(image);
        }


        public async Task InitializeAsync(ContainerBuilder builder)
        {
            try
            {
                builder.RegisterInstance(this).As<ICRUDHistoryRepository<JObject, TEntity, TKey>>();
                await InitializeRuntimeAsync();
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"InitializeAsync error =>", e);
                throw;
            }
        }

        public async Task InitializeRuntimeAsync()
        {
            await using var context = CreateContext();
            await TableInitializer.CreateIfNotExistAsync(context, _config.Table);
            var entities = await context.Set<TEntity>().Include(x => x.Details).ToDictionaryAsync(x => x.Id);
            _cacheService = new CacheService<TKey, TEntity>(new ConcurrentDictionary<TKey, TEntity>(entities), NotFound);
        }


        public async Task<JObject> AddAsync(JObject model, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            await using var context     = CreateContext();
            await using var transaction = await context.Database.BeginTransactionAsync();

            try
            {
                var entity = CreateEntity(model);

                await context.Set<TEntity>().AddAsync(entity);
                await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                entity.Details = CreateDetailEntity(model, entity, true);

                await context.Set<TDetailEntity>().AddAsync(entity.Details);
                await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                _cacheService.Upsert(entity.Id, entity);

                await transaction.CommitAsync();

                return ToModel(entity, returnProperties);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                await transaction.RollbackAsync();

                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public async Task<IEnumerable<JObject>> AddListAsync(JObject[] models, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            await using var context     = CreateContext();
            await using var transaction = await context.Database.BeginTransactionAsync();

            try
            {
                var entities = new List<TEntity>();
                foreach (var model in models)
                {
                    var entity = CreateEntity(model);

                    await context.Set<TEntity>().AddAsync(entity);
                    await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                    entity.Details = CreateDetailEntity(model, entity, true);

                    await context.Set<TDetailEntity>().AddAsync(entity.Details);
                    await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                    _cacheService.Upsert(entity.Id, entity);

                    entities.Add(entity);
                }

                await transaction.CommitAsync();

                return entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                await transaction.RollbackAsync();

                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        //READ

        public Task<IEnumerable<JObject>> GetListAsync(TKey[] ids, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            try
            {
                var entities = _cacheService.GetAll().Where(x => ids.Contains(x.Id));

                return Task.FromResult(entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties)));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public Task<JObject> GetAsync(TKey entityId, string[] returnProperties = null)
        {
            try
            {
                return Task.FromResult(ToModel(_cacheService.Get(entityId), returnProperties));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public Task<(IEnumerable<JObject>, int)> QueryFilterAsync(ConditionGroup @group, OrderBy orderBy, int limit, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            throw new NotImplementedException();
        }

        public Task<(IEnumerable<JObject>, int)> QueryFilterAsync(JConditionGroup group, OrderBy orderBy, int limit, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            try
            {
                var result = _cacheService.GetAll().QuerySimple<TEntity, TKey>(_config, group, orderBy, limit);                

                return Task.FromResult((result.entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties)), result.totalCount));
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                throw GetException(ex);
            }
        }


        public async Task<(IEnumerable<JObject>, int)> QueryAsync(string query, OrderBy orderBy, int limit, string[] returnProperties = null)
        {
            try
            {
                var predicate = await GenericQuery.CreatePredicate<TEntity, TKey>(query, _metadata);
                var (entities, totalCount) = _cacheService.Query(predicate, orderBy, limit);

                return (entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties)), totalCount);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public Task<IEnumerable<JObject>> QueryAsync(Func<TEntity, bool> predicate, string[] returnProperties = null)
        {
            try
            {
                var (entities, _) = _cacheService.Query(predicate, null, 0);

                return Task.FromResult(entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties)));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public Task<IEnumerable<JObject>> GetAllAsync(string[] returnProperties = null)
        {
            try
            {
                var entities = _cacheService.GetAll();

                return Task.FromResult(entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties)));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public Task<IEnumerable<JObject>> GetAllAsync(int limit, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            throw new NotImplementedException();
        }

        //UPDATE
        public async Task<JObject> UpdateAsync(TKey entityId, JObject model, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            try
            {
                await using var context = CreateContext();
                var             entity  = await context.Set<TEntity>().Include(x => x.Details).Include(x => x.HistoryDetails).FirstOrDefaultAsync(x => x.Id.Equals(entityId)) ?? throw NotFound();

                entity         = UpdateEntity(model, entity);
                entity.Details = UpdateDetailEntity(model, entity.Details);

                await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                entity = _cacheService.Upsert(entityId, entity);

                return ToModel(entity, returnProperties);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public async Task<IEnumerable<JObject>> UpdateListAsync(TKey[] ids, JObject[] models, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            try
            {
                await using var context  = CreateContext();
                var             entities = await context.Set<TEntity>().Include(x => x.Details).Include(x => x.HistoryDetails).Where(x => ids.Contains(x.Id)).ToArrayAsync();

                UpdateEntities(models, entities);

                await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                foreach (var entity in entities)
                    _cacheService.Upsert(entity.Id, entity);

                return entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public async Task<IEnumerable<JObject>> UpdateListCreateNewAsync(TKey[] ids, JObject[] models, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            try
            {
                await using var context  = CreateContext();
                var             entities = await context.Set<TEntity>().Include(x => x.Details).Include(x => x.HistoryDetails).Where(x => ids.Contains(x.Id)).ToArrayAsync();

                UpdateCreateEntities(models, entities);

                await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                foreach (var entity in entities)
                    _cacheService.Upsert(entity.Id, entity);

                return entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }


        public async Task<JObject> UpdateCreateNewAsync(TKey entityId, JObject model, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            try
            {
                await using var context = CreateContext();
                var             entity  = await context.Set<TEntity>().Include(x => x.Details).Include(x => x.HistoryDetails).FirstOrDefaultAsync(x => x.Id.Equals(entityId)) ?? throw NotFound();

                entity         = UpdateEntity(model, entity);
                entity.Details = CreateDetailEntity(model, entity, false);

                await context.Set<TDetailEntity>().AddAsync(entity.Details);
                await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                _cacheService.Upsert(entity.Id, entity);

                return ToModel(entity, returnProperties);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        //DELETE
        public async Task DeleteAsync(TKey entityId)
        {
            try
            {
                await using var context = CreateContext();
                var             entity  = await context.Set<TEntity>().FirstOrDefaultAsync(x => x.Id.Equals(entityId)) ?? throw NotFound();

                context.Set<TEntity>().Remove(entity);

                await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                _cacheService.Delete(entity.Id);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public async Task DeleteAllAsync()
        {
            try
            {
                await using var context  = CreateContext();
                var             entities = await context.Set<TEntity>().ToArrayAsync();

                context.Set<TEntity>().RemoveRange(entities);

                await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                foreach (var entity in entities)
                    _cacheService.Delete(entity.Id);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public async Task DeleteListAsync(TKey[] ids)
        {
            try
            {
                await using var context  = CreateContext();
                var             entities = await context.Set<TEntity>().Where(x => ids.Contains(x.Id)).ToArrayAsync();

                context.Set<TEntity>().RemoveRange(entities);

                await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                foreach (var entity in entities)
                    _cacheService.Delete(entity.Id);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }


        public async Task<JObject> ChangeActivityAsync(TKey entityId, bool? deleted)
        {
            try
            {
                await using var context = CreateContext();
                var entity = await context.Set<TEntity>()
                                          .Include(x => x.Details)
                                          .Include(x => x.HistoryDetails)
                                          .FirstOrDefaultAsync(x => x.Id.Equals(entityId))
                             ?? throw NotFound();

                ChangeActivityInternal(entity, deleted);

                await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                _cacheService.Upsert(entity.Id, entity);

                return JObject.FromObject(new
                {
                    Id = entityId,
                    entity.Deleted,
                    entity.Enabled
                });
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }


        public async Task<IEnumerable<JObject>> ChangeActivityListAsync(TKey[] ids, bool? deleted)
        {
            try
            {
                await using var context = CreateContext();
                var entities = await context.Set<TEntity>()
                                            .Include(x => x.Details)
                                            .Include(x => x.HistoryDetails)
                                            .Where(x => ids.Contains(x.Id))
                                            .ToArrayAsync();

                foreach (var entity in entities)
                {
                    ChangeActivityInternal(entity, deleted);
                }

                await context.SaveChangesAsync();

                foreach (var entity in entities)
                    _cacheService.Upsert(entity.Id, entity);

                return entities.Select(x => JObject.FromObject(new
                {
                    x.Id,
                    x.Enabled,
                    x.Deleted
                }));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        private void ChangeActivityInternal(TEntity entity, bool? deleted)
        {
            if (deleted.HasValue)
                entity.Deleted = deleted.Value;
            else
                entity.Enabled = !entity.Enabled;

            entity.Details.Created = DateTime.UtcNow;
        }

        public Task<JObject> GetHistory(TKey entityId, DateTime date, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            try
            {
                var entity  = _cacheService.Get(entityId);
                var details = entity.HistoryDetails.OrderBy(x => x.Created).LastOrDefault(x => x.Created != null && x.Created.Value.Ticks <= date.Ticks);

                if (details == null)
                    throw NotFound();

                return Task.FromResult(ToModel(entity, details, returnProperties));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public Task<JObject[]> GetHistories(TKey entityId, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            try
            {
                var entity = _cacheService.Get(entityId);

                return Task.FromResult(entity.HistoryDetails.Select(x => ToModel(entity, x, returnProperties)).ToArray());
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        private static Exception GetException(Exception e) => e switch
        {
            DbUpdateException ue => ue.InnerException switch
            {
                PostgresException { SqlState: PostgresErrorCodes.UniqueViolation } pe => Duplicate(pe),
                _                                                                     => Error(e)
            },
            SalException => e,
            _            => Error(e)
        };

        private static Exception    NotFound()             => SalError.CreateException($"{typeof(TEntity).Name}NotFound",  $"������ �� �������");
        private static Exception    Duplicate(Exception e) => SalError.CreateException($"{typeof(TEntity).Name}Duplicate", $"������ 㦥 ������� ", innerException: e);
        private static SalException Error(Exception     e) => SalError.CreateException("Error",                            e.Message,                 innerException: e);
    }
}


</details>

    {

        protected abstract JObject                      ToModel(TEntity          entity, string[]  returnProperties);


        protected BaseCRUDDapperSimpleRepository(ModelConfig config, IEnumerable<byte> image)
        {
            _config   = config;
            _metadata = MetadataReference.CreateFromImage(image);
        }

        public async Task InitializeAsync(ContainerBuilder builder)
        {
            try
            {
                builder.RegisterInstance(this).As<ICRUDDapperSimpleRepository<JObject, TEntity, TKey>>();
                await InitializeRuntimeAsync();
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"InitializeAsync error =>", e);
                throw;
            }
        }

        public async Task InitializeRuntimeAsync()
        {
            await using var context = CreateContext();
            await TableInitializer.CreateIfNotExistAsync(context, _config.Table);
        }

        public async Task<JObject> AddAsync(JObject model, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            await using var connection = CreateConnection();
            await connection.OpenAsync();
            await using var transaction = await connection.BeginTransactionAsync();

            try
            {
                var entity = CreateEntity(model);

                var (sql, parameters) = _config.ToInsertData(entity);

                entity.Id = await connection.ExecuteScalarAsync<TKey>(sql, parameters, transaction);

                await transaction.CommitAsync();

                return ToModel(entity, returnProperties);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                await transaction.RollbackAsync();

                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public async Task<IEnumerable<JObject>> AddListAsync(JObject[] models, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            await using var connection = CreateConnection();
            await connection.OpenAsync();
            await using var transaction = await connection.BeginTransactionAsync();

            try
            {
                var entities = new List<TEntity>();
                foreach (var model in models)
                {
                    var entity = CreateEntity(model);

                    var (sql, parameters) = _config.ToInsertData(entity);

                    entity.Id = await connection.ExecuteScalarAsync<TKey>(sql, parameters, transaction);

                    entities.Add(entity);
                }

                await transaction.CommitAsync();

                return entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                await transaction.RollbackAsync();

                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

                var entities = await connection.QueryAsync(query);

                return entities.Select(x => JObject.FromObject((object)x).Transform(returnProperties));
            }
            catch (Exception e)

                var entity = await connection.QueryFirstOrDefaultAsync<TEntity>(sql, parameters) ?? throw NotFound();

                UpdateEntity(model, entity);

                var (update, updateParameters) = _config.ToUpdateData(entity);

                await connection.ExecuteAsync(update, updateParameters);

                return ToModel(entity, returnProperties);
            }
            catch (Exception e)

        {
            await using var connection = CreateConnection();
            await connection.OpenAsync();
            await using var transaction = await connection.BeginTransactionAsync();

            try
            {
                var (sql, parameters) = _config.ToReadListData(ids);

                var entities = (await connection.QueryAsync<TEntity>(sql, parameters) ?? throw NotFound()).ToArray();

                UpdateEntities(models, entities);

                foreach (var entity in entities)
                {
                    var (update, updateParameters) = _config.ToUpdateData(entity);

                    await connection.ExecuteAsync(update, updateParameters, transaction);
                }

                await transaction.CommitAsync();

                return entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties));

        public Task<(IEnumerable<JObject>, int)> QueryFilterAsync(JConditionGroup group, OrderBy orderBy, int limit, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            throw new NotImplementedException();

        public async Task<(IEnumerable<JObject>, int)> QueryFilterAsync(ConditionGroup group, OrderBy orderBy, int limit, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            await using var connection = CreateConnection();

            try
            {
                var entities = await connection.QueryHistoryAsync<TEntity, TDetailEntity, TKey>(_config, group, orderBy, limit);
                var count    = await connection.QueryHistoryCountAsync(_config, group);

                return (entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties)), count);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }


        public async Task<IEnumerable<JObject>> QuerySqlAsync(string query, string[] returnProperties = null)
        {
            await using var connection = CreateConnection();

            try
            {
                query = query.Replace("{table}", $"public.\"{_config.Table}\"")
                             .Replace("{details}", $"public.\"{_config.Table}_details\"");

                var entities = await connection.QueryAsync(query);

                return entities.Select(x => JObject.FromObject((object)x).Transform(returnProperties));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        //UPDATE
        public async Task<JObject> UpdateAsync(TKey entityId, JObject model, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            await using var connection = CreateConnection();
            await connection.OpenAsync();
            await using var transaction = await connection.BeginTransactionAsync();

            try
            {
                //1

                var (sql, parameters) = _config.GetSql(entityId);

                var entity = (await QueryInternalAsync(connection, transaction, sql, parameters)).FirstOrDefault() ?? throw NotFound();

                //2

                entity = UpdateEntity(model, entity);

                var (updateEntitySql, updateEntityParameters) = _config.UpdateEntitySql<TEntity, TKey>(entity);

                await connection.ExecuteAsync(updateEntitySql, updateEntityParameters, transaction);

                //3 

                entity.Details = UpdateDetailEntity(model, entity.Details);

                var (updateDetailEntitySql, updateDetailEntityParameters) = _config.UpdateDetailEntitySql<TDetailEntity, TKey>(entity.Details);

                await connection.ExecuteAsync(updateDetailEntitySql, updateDetailEntityParameters, transaction);


                await transaction.CommitAsync();

                return ToModel(entity, returnProperties);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                await transaction.RollbackAsync();

                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public async Task<IEnumerable<JObject>> UpdateListAsync(TKey[] ids, JObject[] models, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            await using var connection = CreateConnection();
            await connection.OpenAsync();
            await using var transaction = await connection.BeginTransactionAsync();

            try
            {
                var (sql, parameters) = _config.GetListSql(ids);

                var entities = (await QueryInternalAsync(connection, transaction, sql, parameters)).ToArray();

                UpdateEntities(models, entities);

                foreach (var entity in entities)
                {
                    //1

                    var (updateEntitySql, updateEntityParameters) = _config.UpdateEntitySql<TEntity, TKey>(entity);

                    await connection.ExecuteAsync(updateEntitySql, updateEntityParameters, transaction);

                    //2

                    var (updateDetailEntitySql, updateDetailEntityParameters) = _config.UpdateDetailEntitySql<TDetailEntity, TKey>(entity.Details);

                    await connection.ExecuteAsync(updateDetailEntitySql, updateDetailEntityParameters, transaction);
                }

                await transaction.CommitAsync();

                return entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                await transaction.RollbackAsync();

                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public async Task<JObject> UpdateCreateNewAsync(TKey entityId, JObject model, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            await using var connection = CreateConnection();
            await connection.OpenAsync();
            await using var transaction = await connection.BeginTransactionAsync();

            try
            {
                //1

                var (sql, parameters) = _config.GetSql(entityId);

                var entity = (await QueryInternalAsync(connection, transaction, sql, parameters)).FirstOrDefault() ?? throw NotFound();

                //2

                entity = UpdateEntity(model, entity);

                var (updateEntitySql, updateEntityParameters) = _config.UpdateEntitySql<TEntity, TKey>(entity);

                await connection.ExecuteAsync(updateEntitySql, updateEntityParameters, transaction);

                //3 

                entity.Details = CreateDetailEntity(model, entity, false);

                var (insertDetailEntitySql, insertDetailEntityParameters) = _config.InsertDetailEntitySql<TDetailEntity, TKey>(entity.Details);

                entity.Details.DetailId = await connection.ExecuteScalarAsync<long>(insertDetailEntitySql, insertDetailEntityParameters, transaction);

                //4

                var (updateEntityRelationsSql, updateEntityRelationsParameters) = _config.UpdateEntityRelationsSql<TEntity, TKey>(entity, entity.Details.DetailId);

                await connection.ExecuteAsync(updateEntityRelationsSql, updateEntityRelationsParameters, transaction);


                await transaction.CommitAsync();

                return ToModel(entity, returnProperties);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                await transaction.RollbackAsync();

                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public async Task<IEnumerable<JObject>> UpdateListCreateNewAsync(TKey[] ids, JObject[] models, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            await using var connection = CreateConnection();
            await connection.OpenAsync();
            await using var transaction = await connection.BeginTransactionAsync();

            try
            {
                var (sql, parameters) = _config.GetListSql(ids);

                var entities = (await QueryInternalAsync(connection, transaction, sql, parameters)).ToArray();

                UpdateCreateEntities(models, entities);

                foreach (var entity in entities)
                {
                    //1

                    var (updateEntitySql, updateEntityParameters) = _config.UpdateEntitySql<TEntity, TKey>(entity);

                    await connection.ExecuteAsync(updateEntitySql, updateEntityParameters, transaction);

                    //2

                    var (insertDetailEntitySql, insertDetailEntityParameters) = _config.InsertDetailEntitySql<TDetailEntity, TKey>(entity.Details);

                    entity.Details.DetailId = await connection.ExecuteScalarAsync<long>(insertDetailEntitySql, insertDetailEntityParameters, transaction);

                    //3

                    var (updateEntityRelationsSql, updateEntityRelationsParameters) = _config.UpdateEntityRelationsSql<TEntity, TKey>(entity, entity.Details.DetailId);

                    await connection.ExecuteAsync(updateEntityRelationsSql, updateEntityRelationsParameters, transaction);
                }

                await transaction.CommitAsync();

                return entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties));

        public async Task<JObject> ChangeActivityAsync(TKey entityId, bool? deleted)
        {
            await using var connection = CreateConnection();
            try
            {
                var (sql, parameters) = _config.GetSql(entityId);

                var entity = (await QueryInternalAsync(connection, null, sql, parameters)).FirstOrDefault() ?? throw NotFound();

                var resultEntity = await ChangeActivityInternalAsync(connection, null, entity, deleted);

                return ToModel(resultEntity, new[] { "Id", "Enabled", "Deleted" });
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public async Task<IEnumerable<JObject>> ChangeActivityListAsync(TKey[] ids, bool? deleted)
        {
            await using var connection = CreateConnection();
            await connection.OpenAsync();
            await using var transaction = await connection.BeginTransactionAsync();
            try
            {
                var (sql, parameters) = _config.GetListSql(ids);

                var entities = (await QueryInternalAsync(connection, transaction, sql, parameters)).ToArray();

                var resultEntities = new List<TEntity>();

                foreach (var entity in entities)
                {
                    resultEntities.Add(await ChangeActivityInternalAsync(connection, transaction, entity, deleted));
                }

                await transaction.CommitAsync();

                return resultEntities.Select(x => ToModel(x, new[] { "Id", "Enabled", "Deleted" }));
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                await transaction.RollbackAsync();

                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        private async Task<TEntity> ChangeActivityInternalAsync(IDbConnection connection, IDbTransaction transaction, TEntity entity, bool? deleted)
        {
            var (changeActivitySql, changeActivityParameters) = deleted.HasValue
                                                                    ? _config.ChangeDeletedSql<TEntity, TDetailEntity, TKey>(entity, deleted.Value)
                                                                    : _config.ChangeEnabledSql<TEntity, TDetailEntity, TKey>(entity);

            var data = await connection.QuerySingleAsync<TEntity>(changeActivitySql, changeActivityParameters, transaction);

            entity.Enabled = data.Enabled;
            entity.Deleted = data.Deleted;

            return entity;
        }

        private async Task<IEnumerable<TEntity>> QueryInternalAsync(IDbConnection connection, IDbTransaction transaction, string sql, DynamicParameters parameters) =>
            await connection.QueryAsync<TEntity, TDetailEntity, TEntity>(sql, (e, d) =>
            {
                e.Details = d;

                return e;
            }, parameters, transaction, splitOn: "DetailId");

        public async Task<JObject> GetHistory(TKey entityId, DateTime date, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            await using var connection = CreateConnection();
            try
            {
                var (getHistorySql, getHistoryParameters) = _config.GetHistorySql(entityId, date);

                var entity = (await QueryInternalAsync(connection, null, getHistorySql, getHistoryParameters)).FirstOrDefault() ?? throw NotFound();

                return ToModel(entity, returnProperties);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        public async Task<JObject[]> GetHistories(TKey entityId, string[] returnProperties)
        {
            await using var connection = CreateConnection();
            try
            {
                var (getHistoryListSql, getHistoryListParameters) = _config.GetHistoryListSql(entityId);

                var entities = await QueryInternalAsync(connection, null, getHistoryListSql, getHistoryListParameters);

                return entities.Select(x => ToModel(x, returnProperties)).ToArray();
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw GetException(e);
            }
        }

        private static Exception GetException(Exception e) => e switch
        {
            DbUpdateException ue => ue.InnerException switch
            {
                PostgresException { SqlState: PostgresErrorCodes.UniqueViolation } pe => Duplicate(pe),
                _                                                                     => Error(e)
            },
            SalException => e,
            _            => Error(e)
        };

        private static Exception    NotFound()             => SalError.CreateException($"{typeof(TEntity).Name}NotFound",  $"�-������?�? �?�� �?�����?��?��");
        private static Exception    Duplicate(Exception e) => SalError.CreateException($"{typeof(TEntity).Name}Duplicate", $"�-������?�? �?��� �?�?�%��?�'�?�?��' ", innerException: e);
        private static SalException Error(Exception     e) => SalError.CreateException("Error",                            e.Message,                 innerException: e);
    }
}

using SalGenericCRUDHandler.Definitions;
using SalGenericCRUDHandler.Settings;
using SalGenericCRUDHandler.Syntax.Common;

namespace SalGenericCRUDHandler.Syntax.History.Handlers
{
    public class QueryFilter : ISyntax
    {
        private const string CommandName = HandlerNames.QueryFilter;

        public string GetSyntax(ModelConfig model) => model.GetHandler(CommandName , () => $@"
                    var group   = command[""Filter""].ConvertValue<JConditionGroup>();
                    var limit   = {SyntaxHelper.GetValueSyntax("command","int?", "Limit")} ?? 100;
                    var orderBy = command[""OrderBy""].ConvertValue<OrderBy>();
                    var returnProperties = command[""ReturnProperties""]?.Select(x => {SyntaxHelper.GetValueSyntax("x","string")}).ToArray();

                    var (models , totalCount) = await repository.QueryFilterAsync(group , orderBy , limit, returnProperties);

                    return JObject.FromObject(new
                    {{
                         Models = models,
                         TotalCount = totalCount
                    }});         
            ");
    }
}

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

Теперь нужно создать WFM процесс `GenericCRUD.Models.QueryPage`. 

Давайте посмотрим структуру `GenericCRUDModelsAction.cs` ещё раз, чтобы убедиться в паттерне.

Процесс:
- `Start()` → `Config`
- `Config()` — получаем ModelConfig, далее → `QueryPage`
- `QueryPage()` — CRUD стадия, вызывает `GenericCRUD.Action` с `Action: "QueryFilter"` и построенными параметрами. Далее → `Success`
- `Success()` — возвращает результат
- `Failed()` — возвращает ошибку

Входные данные:
```csharp
public class GenericCRUDModelsQueryPageInitObject
{
    public string Model { get; set; }
    public int? Limit { get; set; }
    public int? Offset { get; set; }
    public string Search { get; set; }
    public string SortCol { get; set; }
    public string SortDir { get; set; }
}
```

Context:
```csharp
public class GenericCRUDModelsQueryPageContext
{
    public ModelConfig Config { get; set; }
    public JObject Result { get; set; }
}
```

Result:
```csharp
public class GenericCRUDModelsQueryPageProcessResult { }
```

В `QueryPage()` стадии:
```csharp
public CRUDDefinition QueryPage() => new("Query Page")
{
    GetData = (stage) =>
    {
        stage.Model = InitObject.Model;
        stage.Action = "QueryFilter";

        var limit = InitObject.Limit ?? 25;
        var offset = InitObject.Offset ?? 0;
        var search = InitObject.Search;
        var sortCol = InitObject.SortCol;
        var sortDir = InitObject.SortDir;

        // Validate sortCol against config properties, fallback to KeyName
        var validSortCol = Context.Config.Properties.Any(p => p.Name == sortCol) ? sortCol : Context.Config.KeyName;
        var isDesc = string.Equals(sortDir, "desc", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        // Build filter if search provided
        JObject filter = null;
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(search))
        {
            var textProps = Context.Config.Properties
                .Where(p => p.Type == "string" || p.Type == "DateTime" || p.Type == "DateTime?")
                .ToArray();

            if (textProps.Length > 0)
            {
                var conditions = textProps.Select(p => QB.Condition("string", p.Name, CompareOperator.Contains, search)).ToArray();
                filter = QB.Filter(ConditionOperator.Or, conditions);
            }
        }

        var orderBy = QB.OrderBy(validSortCol, isDesc);

        var data = new JObject();
        if (filter != null)
            data["Filter"] = filter;
        data["Limit"] = limit;
        data["Offset"] = offset;
        data["OrderBy"] = orderBy;

        return data;
    },
    GetNextStage = () =>
    {
        Context.Result = Result;
        return Success;
    },
    GetNextErrorStage = () => Failed
};
```

Но нужно быть осторожным: `QB` доступен через `using WFM.Script.Helpers;`. `ConditionOperator` и `CompareOperator` из `SalGenericCRUDHandler.Implementations.Conditions`.

Давайте проверим, доступны ли `ConditionOperator` и `CompareOperator` в `SalGenericCRUDHandler.Implementations.Conditions`.

</details>
